Let's design custom optimizer using log-domain and exact top-z gradient:

Observation: Since increasing any a_i increases all c_k, under fixed sum we should move mass from coordinates with high influence to active lags to coordinates with low influence. Multiplicative gradient descent does that. It decreases coordinates proportional to gradient g_i = ∑ p_k a_{k-i}. High g_i are positions that contribute to high lags. Over time, creates structure.

FFT scoring functions:
```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a[np.flatnonzero(~np.isfinite(a))]=0
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: a=np.ones_like(a)
    else: a/=s
    return a
```

Let's implement `_conv_self` efficient:
```
def _conv_self(a, L):
    f=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f,L)[:2*len(a)-1]
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c
```
Maybe L stored.

`_score_fast(a,L,m,n): c=_conv_self; return 2*n*c.max()`.

`_grad_p(a,p,L,m,n)`:
```
Frev=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], L)
P=np.fft.rfft(p, L) # p length m padded? rfft(p,L)
h=np.fft.irfft(Frev*P, L)[n-1:n-1+n]
np.maximum(h,0,out=h)
return 2.0*h
```

Line search update:
```
def _mul_update(a, d, eta):
    d=np.maximum(d,0)
    dm=d.max()
    if dm>0: d=d/dm
    # Also add small constant to avoid zeroing? a2 = a * exp(-eta*d)
    a2=np.exp(np.log(a)+(-eta*d)) # a positive; avoid log. Use direct.
    a2=np.where(np.isfinite(a2), a2, 0)
    return normalize(a2)
```

We can combine exact score and p in one iteration. Pseudocode `_soft_refine`:
```
def soft_refine(a, L,m,n,rng, tlimit, beta_start=5, beta_end=500, ...):
    a=normalize(a); deadline=now+tlimit; best=a; best_score=score; beta=beta_start; eta=0.1; stuck=0
    while now<deadline:
       c=conv(a); score=2n c.max()
       if score<best_score: best=a; best_score=score; stuck=0
       else: stuck++
       if stuck>200: # reheat
           prior=random_prior(n,rng)
           mix=0.1+0.3*random
           a=normalize((1-mix)*best + mix*prior)
           c=conv(a); beta=beta_start*?; eta=0.1; stuck=0
           continue
       p, maxc = softmax(c, beta)
       g=grad_p(a,p,L,m,n)
       # also maybe exact top lag gradient mixed
       if iteration%3==0:
           k = argmax c
           g_lag = lag_grad(a,k,L,n)
           g = 0.5*g + 0.5*g_lag
       # line search
       accepted=False
       for eta_try in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25):
           a2=mul_update(a,g,eta_try)
           sc=score_fast(a2)
           if sc<score: a=a2; accepted=True; break
           elif sc<best_score: a=a2; accepted=True; break
       if accepted: eta=min(1.0, eta*1.1)
       else:
           # maybe accept soft improvement
           J=soft_score(c,beta); J2... if J2<J: a=a2
           eta=max(0.02, eta*0.8)
       beta = min(beta_end, beta*1.05)
       it+=1
    return best,best_score
```
Need soft_score for compare maybe not.

But exact score may not improve often when near optimum; if we require exact improvement, may stagnate. We can allow moves that increase exact but decrease high-beta soft objective (approximates lowering top lags while increasing others) hoping later reduce overall. Use simulated annealing:
- Compute current exact score S, candidate S2.
- Accept if S2 < S (or <best). Else accept with probability exp(-(S2-S)/temperature_obj) where temperature_obj maybe 1e-4 decreasing. Since scores around1.5, differences small. This can escape.
But random acceptance may be bad. We can limit.

Maybe use deterministic: accept if soft objective J2 < J, not exact. This minimizes approximate max, can wander. We can still track best exact. Use beta high so soft ~max. J2<J reduces top lags even if another top increases slightly. Over iterations, exact improves.

Detailed `_soft_refine`:
```
def _soft_refine(a, L, n, m, rng, tlimit, alpha_start=1.0, alpha_end=2000.0, eta_start=0.2):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+tlimit
    a=_normalize(a)
    c=_conv_self(a,L); best_score=_score_from_c(c,n); best_a=a.copy()
    alpha=alpha_start; eta=eta_start
    no_accept=0; it=0; alpha_inc=1.03
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       c,_ = _soft? compute c, score, p, J
       if score < best_score - 1e-13: best...
       g=_grad_p(a,p,L,n)
       # occasionally add top lag gradient
       if it % 4 == 3:
          k=int(np.argmax(c))
          glag=_lag_grad(a,k,n)
          # normalize both max1 and mix
          gm = 0.5*_normalize_dir(g)+0.5*glag
          g = _normalize_dir(gm)
       accepted=False
       # try eta values
       last=None
       for et in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1):
           a2=_mul_update(a,g,et)
           c2=_conv_self(a2,L)
           s2=2*n*c2.max()
           # soft J for compare
           v2=alpha*(c2-c2.max()); ... J2
           if J2 < J - 1e-12 or s2 < score - 1e-13:
               if s2 < best_score: best...
               a=a2; best_score?; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
          eta=min(1.2, eta*1.15)
          no_accept=0
       else:
          eta=max(0.02, eta*0.75)
          no_accept+=1
       it+=1
       alpha=min(alpha_end, alpha*alpha_inc)
       if no_accept > 80:
          # reheat: mix with random prior from best
          prior=_random_prior(n,rng)
          mix=0.1+0.4*float(rng.random())
          a=_normalize((1-mix)*best_a + mix*prior)
          eta=eta_start
          alpha=max(alpha_start, alpha*0.4)
          no_accept=0
       if it % 100 == 0 and time... maybe print
    return best_a,best_score
```

```
def _mws_refine(a,L,n,m,rng,tlimit):
   best=a; best_score=score
   deadline...

      prior=random_prior
      x=mws_direction(best,L,n,m,prior,inner=20)
      # line search mix betas

          sc=score_fast(cand)
```

```
       arr=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
```

`_normalize(a, n=None)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
s=float(a.sum())
if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
    a=np.ones_like(a)
    s=float(a.sum())
a = a/s
# ensure positive floor? If we add eps and renorm:
a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
a = a/float(a.sum())
return a
```


`_make_fourier_z(coeffs,n,K)`:
```
z=np.zeros(n)
i=np.arange(n)
for k in range(1,K+1):
    w=2*np.pi*k/n
    z += coeffs[2*k-2]*np.cos(w*i) + coeffs[2*k-1]*np.sin(w*i)
return z
```
This recomputes cos/sin each call. Could precompute? Need top-level global? We can precompute in propose and pass? Functions can't close but can accept array. Simpler recompute; K<=16, n=6121, fine. For gradient, need same cos/sin; recompute.

`_softmax_vec(z)`:
```
z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0)
zmax=z.max()
e=np.exp(z-zmax)
s=e.sum()
if s<=0: return ones/n
a=e/s; return a
```

```
def _soft_objective_from_c(c,n,alpha):
    maxc=float(c.max())
    v=alpha*n*(c-maxc)
    v=np.clip(v,-700,0)
    e=np.exp(v); s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: return alpha*n*maxc
    return float(alpha*n*maxc + math.log(s))
```
This objective scale may be large; L-BFGS okay. But if alpha large, gradient scale large. Use alpha around 20-100.

`_soft_refine` implementation details for speed:
- At each iteration, we need c,p,J. `_soft_info(a,L,n,alpha)` returns c, score, p, J.
- Candidate line search: for each et, a2, info2. This is heavy (3 conv). We can reduce to one et with adaptive based on previous. Use two: eta and eta/2. Maybe enough.
- Instead of gradient of J, we could try `eta` values derived from step size. Since J not guaranteed decrease with multiplicative update due to normalization. Use line search.

Let's implement `_soft_refine` as:
```
def _soft_refine(a,L,n,m,rng,tlimit,alpha_start=5.0,alpha_end=1000.0,eta_start=0.15):
    a=normalize; deadline; best; alpha; eta; noacc; no_best; it=0
    current_info = _soft_info(a,L,n,alpha)
    while now<deadline:
       c, score, p, J = current_info
       if score < best_score - 1e-13:
          best=a; best_score=score; no_best=0
       else: no_best +=1
       # every 200 no_best or max? reheat
       if no_best > 150 or noacc > 60:
          # mix best with random prior
          prior=random_prior
          mix=0.05+0.35*rng.random
          a=normalize((1-mix)*best + mix*prior)
          alpha=max(alpha_start, alpha*0.5)
          eta=eta_start
          noacc=0; no_best=0
          current_info=_soft_info(a,L,n,alpha)
          continue
       g=_grad_p(a,p,L,n)
       # Mix with top lag every 3
       if it % 3 == 2:
          k=int(np.argmax(c))
          glag=_lag_grad(a,k,n)
          gm = _blend_dirs(g, glag, 0.5)
          g=gm
       accepted=False
       # Try 3 step sizes
       for et in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.2*eta):
           if et<=0: continue
           a2=_mul_update(a,g,et)
           c2, s2, p2, J2 = _soft_info(a2,L,n,alpha)
           if J2 < J - 1e-12 or s2 < score - 1e-13:
               a=a2; current_info=(c2,s2,p2,J2); accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
          eta=min(1.5, eta*1.12)
          noacc=0
       else:
          eta=max(0.02, eta*0.8)
          noacc +=1
       alpha=min(alpha_end, alpha*1.04)
       # If alpha high and no soft accept, maybe reduce
       it+=1
       if it % 200 == 0: print?
    # At end, return best. Also try final line from best with small perturbations?
    return best,best_score
```
Need `_soft_info` recompute p/J. For candidate J2, recompute p/J but p2 not used. Could create `_c_score_J(c,n,alpha)` and `_p_from_c(c,alpha)`.

```
    return p,J
```

Pseudo:
```
def _fourier_optimize(n,L,rng,tlimit,K=24,alpha_seq=(5.0,30.0,150.0,600.0),max_restarts=6):
    best_a_list=[]
    idxs=np.arange(n)
    deadline=...
    for r in range(max_restarts):
        x = rng.normal(0, 0.2, 2*K)
        # maybe include larger first mode: x[1]=rng.choice([-1,1])*0.5? 
        for alpha in alpha_seq:
            if now>deadline-30: break
            try:
                res=opt.minimize(_fourier_value, x, jac=_fourier_grad, args=(n,L,float(alpha),K), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(-3,3)]*(2*K), options={'maxiter':80,'maxfun':250})
                x=res.x
            except: pass
        z=_make_fourier_z(x,n,c,K)
        a=_softmax_vec(z)
        sc=_score_fast(a,L,n)
        add to list
    return best list
```

`_softmax_p_from_c(c, beta)` where beta = alpha*n? Maybe pass beta.
```
def _p_and_J(c, beta):
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    v=beta*(c-maxc)
    v=np.clip(v,-700.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(v)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       p=np.zeros_like(c); p[np.argmax(c)]=1.0; J=beta*maxc
    else:
       p=e/s; J=float(beta*maxc + math.log(s))
    return p,J
```
Here beta already includes n*alpha. In soft_refine, use `beta = alpha * n`.

`_grad_p(a,p,L,n)`:
```
Frev=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], L)
P=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
h=np.fft.irfft(Frev*P, L)
g=h[n-1:n-1+n].copy()
np.maximum(g,0,out=g)
return 2.0*g
```
Check p length m<=L. Good.

`_lag_grad(a,k,n)`:
```
g=np.zeros(n)
lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
if lo<=hi:
   idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
   src=k-idx
   g[idx]=2.0*a[src]
return g
```

`_dir_norm(d)`:
```
d=np.asarray(d,float); d=np.where(np.isfinite(d),d,0); np.maximum(d,0,out=d)
mx=d.max(); if mx>0: d=d/mx; return d
```

`_blend_dirs(d1,d2,w)`:
```
d1=_dir_norm(d1); d2=_dir_norm(d2); d=(1-w)*d1+w*d2; return _dir_norm(d)
```

`_mul_update(a,d,eta)`:
```
d=_dir_norm(d)
a2=a*np.exp(-eta*d)
a2=np.where(np.isfinite(a2), a2, 0.0)
return _normalize(a2)
```

`_soft_refine` code:
```
def _soft_refine(a, L, n, rng, tlimit, beta_start=None, beta_end=None, eta_start=0.15):
    if beta_start is None: beta_start=10.0*n
    if beta_end is None: beta_end=1000.0*n
    a=_normalize(a)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+tlimit
    c,score,p,J=_soft_info(a,L,n,beta_start)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    beta=beta_start; eta=eta_start; no_acc=0; no_best=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c,score,p,J = _soft_info(a,L,n,beta)
       if score < best_score - 1e-13:
           best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_best=0
       else:
           no_best +=1
       if no_best > 120 or no_acc > 80:
           prior=_random_prior(n,rng)
           mix=0.05+0.35*float(rng.random())
           a=_normalize((1.0-mix)*best_a + mix*prior)
           beta=max(beta_start, beta*0.5)
           eta=eta_start
           no_acc=0; no_best=0
           it=0
           continue
       g=_grad_p(a,p,L,n)
       if it % 3 == 2:
           k=int(np.argmax(c))
           glag=_lag_grad(a,k,n)
           g=_blend_dirs(g, glag, 0.35)
       accepted=False
       steps=(eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta)
       for et in steps:
           if et <= 1e-6: continue
           a2=_mul_update(a,g,et)
           c2,s2,p2,J2=_soft_info(a2,L,n,beta)
           if J2 < J - 1e-12 or s2 < score - 1e-13:
               a=a2
               if s2 < best_score:
                   best_score=s2; best_a=a2.copy()
                   no_best=0
               accepted=True
               break
       if accepted:
           eta=min(1.5, eta*1.12); no_acc=0
       else:
           eta=max(0.02, eta*0.8); no_acc +=1
       beta=min(beta_end, beta*1.04)
       it +=1
       if it == 200: # maybe print
          pass
    # final: maybe re-evaluate best with current beta? not needed
    return best_a, best_score
```
Potential inefficiency: At top loop we recompute `_soft_info`; after candidate accept we already have c2 etc but next iteration recomputes. Fine.

If no_acc high, we may never accept because threshold strict. Could allow softer threshold `J2 < J` only; exact not needed. We use or. If J2 slightly greater due to numerical, no accept. Use `-1e-10`.

Soft J with beta high may be flat; gradient may not reduce. Reheat helps.

```

def _convolve_ff(a,b,L,m):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); B=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,L)[:m]
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c
```

Random prior implementation high-level:
```
def _random_prior(n,rng):
    typ=int(rng.integers(6))
    if typ==0:
       return _normalize(rng.exponential(1.0,n))
    if typ==1:
       sh=float(rng.uniform(0.3,3.0))
       return _normalize(rng.gamma(sh,1.0,n))
    if typ==2:
       z=rng.normal(0,1,n); return _normalize(np.abs(z)+1e-6)
    if typ==3:
       # smooth log random
       K=int(rng.integers(3,12))
       z=zeros(n); i=arange(n)
       for k in 1..K:
          amp=rng.normal(0,1.0/max(1,k**0.5))
          ph=rng.uniform(0,2pi)
          z += amp*np.cos(2πk i/n+ph)
       z += rng.normal(0,0.2,n)
       return _normalize(np.exp(z - z.max()))
    if typ==4:
       # additive sine profiles with random rho/phase and second harmonic
       rho=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2pi))
       rho2=float(rng.uniform(0,0.3)); ph2=...
       x = 1 + rho*np.sin(2πi/n+ph) + rho2*np.sin(4πi/n+ph2)
       return _normalize(np.maximum(0,x))
    # typ5: mixture of two priors
       return _normalize(0.5*_random_prior(n,rng)+0.5*... )
```
This recursively could call itself; no closures. Need set recursion limit? typ5 probability 1/6, ok but could recurse. Avoid recursion by generating two types separately. Use helper `_random_prior_base` no typ5? Simpler no recursive.

Potential issue: additive sine with rho up to0.6 positive? min 1-0.9=0.1. Good.

Initial deterministic candidates:
- uniform: ones
- linear decreasing/increasing: i, n-i
- arcsine: sin(pi i/(n-1))
- sine additive as above.
But not essential.

Now, code details for sorting pool:
```
def _add_candidate(arrays, scores, arr, score, max_len=16):
    if arr is None: return
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    arr=np.asarray(arr,float).copy(); arr=_normalize(arr)
    if len(scores) < max_len:
       arrays.append(arr); scores.append(float(score))
    else:
       worst=max(scores); if score < worst:
          idx=scores.index(worst); arrays[idx]=arr; scores[idx]=float(score)
```

`_normalize` takes array; if array is list, len. Need if array length 0. Return ones(n) if n? But `_normalize` doesn't know n if input empty. We can pass expected n. Define `_normalize(a, n=None)`: if n is None, n=a.size. Use in internal. For `height_sequence_1` resample. Simpler:
```
def _normalize(a, n):
   arr=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if arr.size != n: arr=_resample_to_length(arr,n)
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
   arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
   s=float(arr.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
      arr=np.ones(n)
   else:
      arr=arr/s
   arr=np.maximum(arr,1e-300)
   arr=arr/float(arr.sum())
   return arr
```
Call with n. For `_mul_update`, call `_normalize(a2,n)`. Good.

`_resample_to_length(arr,n)`:
```
def _resample_to_length(arr,n):
   arr=np.asarray(arr,float).ravel()
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
   if arr.size == n: return arr
   if arr.size == 0: return np.ones(n)
   if arr.size == 1: return np.full(n, arr[0])
   old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size)
   new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   return np.interp(new,old,arr)
```

`_softmax_vec`:
```
def _softmax_vec(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
   z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0.0)
   zmax=float(np.max(z))
   e=np.exp(z-zmax)
   s=float(e.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
      return np.ones_like(z)/z.size
   return e/s
```
No positivity floor; zeros possible if underflow but okay; later if used in normalize not. For final sequences, `_normalize` after a maybe ensures floor. But if a from softmax has underflow zeros, objective may have zeros; okay. Could add tiny floor: `e=np.maximum(e,1e-300)`. Good.

```
def _stable_softmax(z, clip=50.0):
   z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
   z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0)
   m=np.max(z)
   z=z-m
   z=np.clip(z,-clip,0)
   e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: uniform; else return e/s
```

MWS:
```
def _mws_direction(f,L,n,m,prior,inner=20,eta_start=5.0,eta_end=50.0):
    f=_normalize(f,n); prior=_normalize(prior,n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,L)
    Frev=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1],L)
    x=prior.copy()
    y=np.full(m,1.0/float(m))
    logp=np.log(np.maximum(prior,1e-300))
    avg=np.zeros(n); count=0
    for it in range(inner):
       frac=0 if inner<=1 else it/(inner-1)
       eta=eta_start+(eta_end-eta_start)*frac
       u=_convolve_ff(f,x,L,m)
       vu=n*u
       vma=float(np.max(vu))
       z=eta*(vu-vma)
       y=_stable_softmax(z,50.0)
       avg += x; count +=1
       s = 0.5 * _grad_p(f,y,L,n)  # h
       ps=n*s
       pma=float(np.max(ps))
       zx = -eta*(ps-pma) + 0.8*logp
       x=_stable_softmax(zx,50.0)
    if count>0:
       return _normalize(0.5*(avg/float(count)) + 0.5*x, n)
    return x
```
Is `s = 0.5*grad_p` correct? `_grad_p` returns 2∑ p_k f_{k-i}. We need h=∑ p_k f_{k-i}. Yes.

In MWS, `u=_convolve_ff(f,x)` computes f*x linear convolution. Constraint f*b≤M. Then set y weights on constraints. Next update x based on s = ∑ y_k f_{k-i}. This decreases x where constraints tight and f contributes. Good.

```
def _fourier_optimize(n,L,rng,tlimit,K=20, alpha_values=(5,20,80,300), max_restarts=6):
    deadline=time.monotonic()+tlimit
    results=[]
    for r in range(max_restarts):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline-30: break
       x = rng.normal(0, 0.25, 2*K)
       # ensure at least one meaningful coefficient
       if np.max(np.abs(x)) < 0.05:
           x[1] += 0.2
       for alpha in alpha_values:
           if time.monotonic()>deadline-15: break
           try:
              bounds=[(-3.0,3.0)]*(2*K)
              options={'maxiter':60, 'maxfun':200, 'ftol':1e-12}
              res=opt.minimize(_fourier_value, x, jac=_fourier_grad, args=(n,L,float(alpha),K), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
              if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
                 x=np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel()
           except Exception:
              pass
       z=_make_fourier_z(x,n,K)
       a=_softmax_vec(z)
       sc=_score_fast(a,L)
       if np.isfinite(sc): results.append((a,sc))
    # sort by score using argsort
    if not results: return []
    scores=[s for _,s in results] # list comprehension okay? It's fine, not lambda? List comprehension is allowed? The rule says no lambda functions; comprehensions okay. But to be safe maybe loop.
```
List comprehension is not lambda, should be okay. But some strict? Use loops to be safe.

```
    scores=[]; order? 
    for a,s in results: ...
    order=np.argsort(scores)
    return [(results[i][0], results[i][1]) for i in order] # comprehension. OK.
```

```
def _random_prior(n, rng):
    typ = int(rng.integers(6))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if typ == 0:
       return _normalize(rng.exponential(1.0, size=n), n)
    if typ == 1:
       sh = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0))
       return _normalize(rng.gamma(sh, 1.0, size=n), n)
    if typ == 2:
       z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
       arr = np.abs(z) + 1e-6
       return _normalize(arr, n)
    if typ == 3:
       K = int(rng.integers(3, 13))
       z = np.zeros(n)
       for k in range(1, K+1):
          amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / max(1, math.sqrt(float(k)))))
          ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*math.pi))
          z += amp * np.cos(2.0*math.pi*k*i/float(n) + ph)
       z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.2))*np.ones(n)
       z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, size=n)
```

```
def _normalize(a, n):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != n:
       arr = _resample_to_length(arr, n)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
       arr = arr / s
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-300)
    arr = arr / float(arr.sum())
    return arr
```

```
    s=float(e.sum())

    return e/s
```

```
def _p_and_J(c, beta):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    v=beta*(c-maxc)
    v=np.clip(v, -700.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(v)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       p=np.zeros_like(c); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
       return p, float(beta*maxc)
    p=e/s
    J=float(beta*maxc + math.log(s))
    return p,J
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing

    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2)

The program combines several ideas:

1. Keep the internal representation normalized to sum 1.
2. Use FFT-based scorers and gradients for the soft log-sum-exp surrogate
   of max(conv(a,a)).
3. Use a multiplicative gradient/refinement loop with annealed temperature,
   occasional top-lag subgradient boosts, and reheating.
4. Use a second refinement based on multiplicative-weights iterations for
   the linear best-response LP direction from the state-of-the-art method.
5. Use low-dimensional Fourier parameterization plus L-BFGS (when SciPy is
   available) to produce diverse smooth profiles, then refine them in the
   full-dimensional space.
6. Restart from the previous state-of-the-art sequence if available.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _sp_minimize
except Exception:
    _sp_minimize = None


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic helpers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _fft_length(n):
    m = 2 * int(n) - 2
    if m <= 0:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _resample_to_length(arr, n):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    if arr.size == n:
        return arr.copy()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 1:
        return np.full(int(n), float(arr[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.interp(new, old, arr).astype(np.float64)


def _normalize(a, n):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != int(n):
        arr = _resample_to_length(arr, n)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        arr = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        arr = arr / s
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-300)
    arr = arr / float(arr.sum())
    return arr


def _softmax_vec(z):
    arr = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    zmax = float(np.max(arr))
    e = np.exp(arr - zmax)
    e = np.maximum(e, 1e-300)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones_like(arr, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return e / s


def _stable_softmax(raw, clip=50.0):
    z = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(z))
    z = z - m
    z = np.clip(z, -clip, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones_like(z, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# FFT convolution helpers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _conv_self(a, L):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if int(L) < m:
        L = _fft_length(n)
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, int(L))
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, int(L))[:m]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _convolve_ff(a, b, L, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, int(L))
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, int(L))
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, int(L))[:m]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _conv_precomp(F, b, L, m):
    B = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64), int(L))
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * B, int(L))[:m]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_fast(a, L):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    c = _conv_self(a, int(L))
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float(np.inf)
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mx


def _p_and_J(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    v = float(beta) * (c - maxc)
    v = np.clip(v, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p, float(float(beta) * maxc)
    p = e / s
    J = float(float(beta) * maxc + math.log(s))
    return p, J


def _soft_info(a, L, n, beta):
    c = _conv_self(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), int(L))
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    p, J = _p_and_J(c, float(beta))
    return c, 2.0 * float(n) * mx, p, J


def _soft_objective_from_c(c, n, alpha):
    beta = float(alpha) * float(n)
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    v = beta * (c - maxc)
    v = np.clip(v, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float(beta * maxc)
    return float(beta * maxc + math.log(s))


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Gradients / directions
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _grad_precomp(Frev, p, L, n):
    P = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), int(L))
    h = np.fft.irfft(Frev * P, int(L))
    g = h[n - 1:n - 1 + n].copy()
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    return 2.0 * g


def _grad_p(a, p, L, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    Frev = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], int(L))
    return _grad_precomp(Frev, p, int(L), int(n))


def _lag_grad(a, k, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    lo = max(0, int(k) - (int(n) - 1))
    hi = min(int(n) - 1, int(k))
    if lo <= hi:
        idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        src = np.asarray(int(k) - idx, dtype=np.int64)
        g[idx] = 2.0 * a[src]
    return g


def _dir_norm(d):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
    mx = float(d.max())
    if mx > 0.0 and np.isfinite(mx):
        d = d / mx
    return d


def _blend_dirs(d1, d2, w):
    d1 = _dir_norm(d1)
    d2 = _dir_norm(d2)
    w = float(w)
    d = (1.0 - w) * d1 + w * d2
    return _dir_norm(d)


def _mul_update(a, d, eta, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    d = _dir_norm(d)
    eta = float(eta)
    if eta <= 0.0:
        return _normalize(a, n)
    a2 = a * np.exp(-eta * d)
    a2 = np.where(np.isfinite(a2), a2, 0.0)
    return _normalize(a2, n)


def _smooth_vector(a, w, n):
    a = _normalize(a, n)
    w = int(max(1, w))
    if a.size <= w:
        return a
    k = np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64) / float(w)
    y = np.convolve(a, k, mode="same")
    return _normalize(y, n)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Random and deterministic starts
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _random_prior(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    typ = int(rng.integers(6))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if typ == 0:
        return _normalize(rng.exponential(1.0, size=n), n)

    if typ == 1:
        sh = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0))
        return _normalize(rng.gamma(sh, 1.0, size=n), n)

    if typ == 2:
        z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
        return _normalize(np.abs(z) + 1e-6, n)

    if typ == 3:
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 13))
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, K + 1):
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / max(1.0, math.sqrt(float(k)))))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * k * i / float(n) + ph)
        z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.2)) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, size=n)
        return _softmax_vec(z)

    if typ == 4:
        rho1 = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.7))
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        rho2 = float(rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        arr = (
            1.0
            + rho1 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * i / float(n) + ph1)
            + rho2 * np.sin(4.0 * math.pi * i / float(n) + ph2)
        )
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        return _normalize(arr, n)

    # Mixed iid start
    a1 = _normalize(rng.exponential(1.0, size=n), n)
    sh = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0))
    a2 = _normalize(rng.gamma(sh, 1.0, size=n), n)
    t = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
    return _normalize((1.0 - t) * a1 + t * a2, n)


def _initial_candidates(n, rng, L):
    n = int(n)
    raw = []

    base = None
    try:
        base = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821  (global provided by runner)
    except NameError:
        base = None

    if base is not None:
        b = _normalize(base, n)
        raw.append(b)
        raw.append(b[::-1])
        raw.append(0.5 * b + 0.5 * b[::-1])

    raw.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if n > 1:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        t = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)

    raw.append(t)
    raw.append(1.0 - t)
    raw.append(np.sin(math.pi * t))
    raw.append(np.exp(-((t - 0.25) ** 2) / 0.05) + np.exp(-((t - 0.75) ** 2) / 0.05))

    for rho in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8):
        for ph in (0.0, math.pi):
            raw.append(1.0 + rho * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * t + ph))
            raw.append(1.0 + rho * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * t + ph))

    for _ in range(36):
        raw.append(_random_prior(n, rng))

    if base is not None:
        b = _normalize(base, n)
        for _ in range(10):
            p = _random_prior(n, rng)
            tt = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.40))
            raw.append((1.0 - tt) * b + tt * p)

    arrays = []
    scores = []
    for arr in raw:
        a = _normalize(arr, n)
        sc = _score_fast(a, L)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            arrays.append(a)
            scores.append(float(sc))

    if not arrays:
        arrays.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
        scores.append(_score_fast(arrays[0], L))

    order = np.argsort(scores)
    out = []
    for idx in order[:12]:
        out.append(arrays[int(idx)])
    return out


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Full-dimensional soft/refinement search
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _soft_refine(a, L, n, rng, tlimit, beta_start=None, beta_end=None, eta_start=0.15):
    n = int(n)
    a = _normalize(a, n)
    if beta_start is None or not np.isfinite(beta_start) or beta_start <= 0.0:
        beta_start = 10.0 * float(n)
    if beta_end is None or beta_end <= beta_start:
        beta_end = max(beta_start * 1.1, 1000.0 * float(n))
    eta = float(eta_start)
    beta = float(beta_start)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max(1e-6, tlimit))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(a, L)
    beta = float(min(beta, best_score_exact_beta_end(beta_end)))

    no_acc = 0
    no_best = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c, score, p, J = _soft_info(a, L, n, beta)

        if score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_best = 0
        else:
            no_best += 1

        if no_acc > 70 or no_best > 260:
            prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
            mix = 0.05 + 0.35 * float(rng.random())
            a = _normalize((1.0 - mix) * best_a + mix * prior, n)
            beta = max(float(beta_start), float(beta) * 0.45)
            eta = float(eta_start)
            no_acc = 0
            no_best = 0
            it = 0
            continue

        g = _grad_p(a, p, L, n)

        # Periodically reinforce the current worst lag with a pure subgradient.
        if it % 3 == 2:
            k = int(np.argmax(c))
            glag = _lag_grad(a, k, n)
            g = _blend_dirs(g, glag, 0.35)

        accepted = False
        steps = (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta)
        for st in steps:
            if st <= 1e-8:
                continue
            a2 = _mul_update(a, g, st, n)
            c2, s2, p2, J2 = _soft_info(a2, L, n, beta)
            if (J2 < J - 1e-12) or (s2 < score - 1e-13):
                a = a2
                if s2 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(s2)
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    no_best = 0
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            eta = min(1.5, eta * 1.12)
            no_acc = 0
        else:
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.80)
            no_acc += 1

        beta = min(float(beta_end), beta * 1.04)
        it += 1

    return best_a, best_score


def _soft_refine_high(a, L, n, rng, tlimit):
    return _soft_refine(
        a,
        L,
        n,
        rng,
        tlimit,
        beta_start=200.0 * float(n),
        beta_end=3000.0 * float(n),
        eta_start=0.08,
    )


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Multiplicative-weights / LP-direction refinement
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _mws_direction(f, L, n, m, prior, inner=18, eta_start=5.0, eta_end=50.0):
    f = _normalize(f, n)
    prior = _normalize(prior, n)
    inner = max(1, int(inner))

    F = np.fft.rfft(f, int(L))
    Frev = np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], int(L))

    x = prior.copy()
    y = np.full(int(m), 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    log_prior = np.log(np.maximum(prior, 1e-300))
    avg = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    count = 0

    for it in range(inner):
        frac = 0.0 if inner <= 1 else float(it) / float(inner - 1)
        eta = float(eta_start) + (float(eta_end) - float(eta_start)) * frac

        u = _conv_precomp(F, x, L, m)
        vu = float(n) * u
        vma = float(np.max(vu))
        z = eta * (vu - vma)
        y = _stable_softmax(z, 50.0)

        avg += x
        count += 1

        s = 0.5 * _grad_precomp(Frev, y, L, n)
        ps = float(n) * s
        pma = float(np.max(ps))
        zx = -eta * (ps - pma) + 0.8 * log_prior
        x = _stable_softmax(zx, 50.0)

    if count > 0:
        return _normalize(0.5 * (avg / float(count)) + 0.5 * x, n)
    return x


def _mws_refine(a, L, n, rng, tlimit):
    n = int(n)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    a = _normalize(a, n)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max(1e-6, tlimit))
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(best, L)
    inner = 18
    if tlimit > 80.0:
        inner = 26

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
        x = _mws_direction(best, L, n, m, prior, inner=inner, eta_start=4.0, eta_end=60.0)
        xs = _smooth_vector(x, 5, n)

        for t in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40, 0.70, 1.0):
            cand = _normalize((1.0 - t) * best + t * xs, n)
            sc = _score_fast(cand, L)
            if sc < best_score:
                best = cand
                best_score = float(sc)

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break

    return best, best_score


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Low-dimensional Fourier parametrization
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _make_fourier_z(coeffs, n, K):
    coeffs = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if coeffs.size != 2 * int(K):
        coeffs = np.resize(coeffs, 2 * int(K))
    coeffs = np.where(np.isfinite(coeffs), coeffs, 0.0)
    n = int(n)
    K = int(K)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        w = 2.0 * math.pi * float(k) / float(n)
        c1 = float(coeffs[2 * k - 2])
        c2 = float(coeffs[2 * k - 1])
        z += c1 * np.cos(w * i) + c2 * np.sin(w * i)
    return z


def _fourier_value(x, n, L, alpha, K):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != 2 * int(K):
        x = np.resize(x, 2 * int(K))
    z = _make_fourier_z(x, n, K)
    a = _softmax_vec(z)
    c = _conv_self(a, L)
    return _soft_objective_from_c(c, n, alpha)


def _fourier_grad(x, n, L, alpha, K):
    n = int(n)
    K = int(K)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != 2 * K:
        x = np.resize(x, 2 * K)
    z = _make_fourier_z(x, n, K)
    a = _softmax_vec(z)
    c = _conv_self(a, L)
    beta = float(alpha) * float(n)
    p, _ = _p_and_J(c, beta)
    g2 = _grad_p(a, p, L, n)
    ga = beta * g2
    ag = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gz = a * ga - a * ag

    grad = np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        w = 2.0 * math.pi * float(k) / float(n)
        cosb = np.cos(w * i)
        sinb = np.sin(w * i)
        grad[2 * k - 2] = float(np.dot(gz, cosb))
        grad[2 * k - 1] = float(np.dot(gz, sinb))
    return grad


def _fourier_optimize(n, L, rng, tlimit, K=20, alpha_values=(5.0, 25.0, 100.0, 400.0), max_restarts=6):
    if _sp_minimize is None:
        return []
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max(1e-6, tlimit))
    results = []

    for r in range(max_restarts):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break
        x = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=2 * K)
        if float(np.max(np.abs(x))) < 0.05:
            x[1] += 0.2

        for alpha in alpha_values:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                break
            try:
                bounds = [(-3.0, 3.0)] * (2 * K)
                options = {"maxiter": 60, "maxfun": 220, "ftol": 1e-12}
                res = _sp_minimize(
                    _fourier_value,
                    np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64),
                    jac=_fourier_grad,
                    args=(int(n), int(L), float(alpha), int(K)),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options=options,
                )
                if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
                    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            except Exception:
                pass

        z = _make_fourier_z(x, n, K)
        a = _softmax_vec(z)
        sc = _score_fast(a, L)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            results.append((a, float(sc)))

    if not results:
        return []

    rscores = []
    for item in results:
        rscores.append(float(item[1]))
    order = np.argsort(rscores)
    out = []
    for idx in order:
        out.append(results[int(idx)])
    return out


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Candidate pool helpers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _add_candidate(arrays, scores, arr, score, max_len=16):
    if arr is None:
        return
    try:
        score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    n = int(np.asarray(arr).size)
    a = _normalize(arr, n)
    if len(scores) < int(max_len):
        arrays.append(a)
        scores.append(score)
    else:
        worst = max(scores)
        if score < worst:
            idx = scores.index(worst)
            arrays[idx] = a
            scores[idx] = score


def _soft_refine_beta_cap(beta_end):
    # Small helper to keep explicit and non-constant.
    return max(1.0, float(beta_end))


def best_score_exact_beta_end(beta_end):
    # Placeholder to satisfy a prior local name in code evolution.
    return float(max(1.0, float(beta_end)))


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exact evaluation wrappers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _internal_exact(seq):
    if not isinstance(seq, list) or len(seq) == 0:
        return float(np.inf)
    a = []
    for x in seq:
        try:
            y = float(x)
        except Exception:
            return float(np.inf)
        if math.isnan(y) or math.isinf(y):
            return float(np.inf)
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        if y > 1000.0:
            y = 1000.0
        a.append(y)
    n = len(a)
    s = float(sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return float(np.inf)
    b = np.convolve(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(b)) / (s * s))


def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)  # noqa: F821  (provided by runner)
    except NameError:
        val = _internal_exact(seq)
    except Exception:
        val = _internal_exact(seq)
    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        val = float(np.inf)
    return val


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main entry point
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 1.0:
        budget = 2400.0

    hard_deadline = start + max(5.0, budget - 2.0)
    if budget >= 120.0:
        search_deadline = start + max(10.0, budget - 45.0)
    else:
        search_deadline = start + max(5.0, budget - 5.0)
    if search_deadline > hard_deadline - 5.0:
        search_deadline = hard_deadline - 5.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Use 6121 to stay compatible with the previous state-of-the-art length.
    n = 6121
    L = _fft_length(n)
    m = 2 * n - 1

    pool_arrays = []
    pool_scores = []

    inits = _initial_candidates(n, rng, L)
    for a in inits:
        sc = _score_fast(a, L)
        _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, a, sc, 16)

    if not pool_arrays:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        seq = [float(x) for x in a]
        return seq

    order = np.argsort(pool_scores)
    global_best_idx = int(order[0])
    global_best_arr = pool_arrays[global_best_idx].copy()
    global_best_score = float(pool_scores[global_best_idx])

    print("initial best internal score = %.10f" % global_best_score, flush=True)

    # Low-dimensional Fourier global-ish starts.
    remaining = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if _sp_minimize is not None and budget > 260.0 and remaining > 120.0:
        fbudget = min(420.0, max(30.0, 0.18 * remaining))
        four = _fourier_optimize(n, L, rng, fbudget, K=20,
                                 alpha_values=(5.0, 25.0, 100.0, 400.0),
                                 max_restarts=7)
        for a, sc in four:
            sc2 = _score_fast(a, L)
            _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, a, sc2, 16)
        if pool_scores:
            bidx = int(np.argmin(pool_scores))
            if pool_scores[bidx] < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = float(pool_scores[bidx])
                global_best_arr = pool_arrays[bidx].copy()
        print("after fourier best internal score = %.10f" % global_best_score, flush=True)

    method = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < search_deadline:
        rem = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 20.0:
            break
        if not pool_scores:
            break

        best_idx = int(np.argmin(pool_scores))

        if len(pool_arrays) > 1 and float(rng.random()) < 0.22:
            cand_idx = int(rng.integers(len(pool_arrays)))
            cand = pool_arrays[cand_idx].copy()
        else:
            cand = pool_arrays[best_idx].copy()

        if float(rng.random()) < 0.35:
            prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
            t = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.22))
            cand = _normalize((1.0 - t) * cand + t * prior, n)

        method = int(method)
        if method % 3 == 0:
            lim = min(220.0, max(20.0, 0.30 * rem))
            newa, news = _soft_refine(cand, L, n, rng, lim)
        elif method % 3 == 1:
            lim = min(140.0, max(20.0, 0.20 * rem))
            newa, news = _mws_refine(cand, L, n, rng, lim)
        else:
            lim = min(140.0, max(20.0, 0.20 * rem))
            newa, news = _soft_refine_high(cand, L, n, rng, lim)

        _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, newa, news, 16)

        if np.isfinite(news) and news < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = float(news)
            global_best_arr = newa.copy()
            print("new internal best = %.10f after %.1fs" % (global_best_score, time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)

        # Occasionally create a novelty in the pool by mixing two top survivors.
        if len(pool_arrays) >= 2 and method % 5 == 0:
            o = np.argsort(pool_scores)
            i0 = int(o[0])
            i1 = int(o[1])
            t = float(rng.uniform(0.20, 0.80))
            mix = _normalize((1.0 - t) * pool_arrays[i0] + t * pool_arrays[i1], n)
            mixsc = _score_fast(mix, L)
            _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, mix, mixsc, 16)

        method += 1

        now = time.monotonic()
        if now - last_print > 60.0:
            last_print = now
            cur_best = float(np.min(pool_scores)) if pool_scores else global_best_score
            print("%.1fs elapsed, current pool best = %.10f, global = %.10f" %
                  (now - start, cur_best, global_best_score), flush=True)

    # Final exact verification of a few best internal candidates.
    if pool_scores:
        order = np.argsort(pool_scores)
    else:
        order = np.array([], dtype=np.int64)

    eval_candidates = []
    evaluated_ids = set()

    max_evals = 6 if budget > 1500.0 else 3
    for j in range(min(max_evals, len(order))):
        cand = pool_arrays[int(order[j])]
        oid = id(cand)
        if oid not in evaluated_ids:
            evaluated_ids.add(oid)
            eval_candidates.append(cand)

    ob = id(global_best_arr)
    if ob not in evaluated_ids:
        evaluated_ids.add(ob)
        eval_candidates.append(global_best_arr)

    best_seq = None
    best_exact = float(np.inf)

    for cand in eval_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 3.0:
            break
        seq = []
        for x in cand:
            y = float(x)
            if not np.isfinite(y) or y < 0.0:
                y = 0.0
            if y > 1.0:
                y = 1.0
            seq.append(y)
        val = _exact_score(seq)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
            best_exact = float(val)
            best_seq = seq

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = []
        for x in global_best_arr:
            y = float(x)
            if not np.isfinite(y) or y < 0.0:
                y = 0.0
            best_seq.append(y)

    print("final best exact score = %.10f" % best_exact, flush=True)
    return best_seq
```
